([lukaskellerstein/claude-my-marketplace](https://github.com/lukaskellerstein/claude-my-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/dev-tools-plugin/skills/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Facilitate structured brainstorming sessions to generate, explore, and refine ideas before committing to requirements. Use when the user has a vague concept, wants to explore possibilities, needs creative solutions, or is in the early ideation phase. Do NOT use when the requirements are already clear and the user is ready to plan implementation (use create-prd or prd-to-tasks instead). ([marco-souza/skills](https://github.com/marco-souza/skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Interactive idea maturation — explore, research, shape raw ideas into actionable plans. Use when you have a rough idea and want to think it through. ([martineserios/thebrana](https://github.com/martineserios/thebrana/tree/HEAD/system/skills/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Multi-agent ideation against a free-form prompt. Wraps `jutsu swarm brainstorm "&lt;prompt&gt;"` to run claude / codex / antigravity in parallel with three different angles (claude=long-horizon framing, codex=code-pattern grounding, antigravity=cross-domain analogy), synthesizes into a ranked list of approaches with tradeoffs. Use when the user says "brainstorm", "ideas for", "how should we approach", "options for", or invokes /brainstorm. Designed to feed into `decide` (for ADR capture of the picked option) or `refactor-plan` (for execution of the picked option). ([momentmaker/kaijutsu](https://github.com/momentmaker/kaijutsu/tree/HEAD/skills/core/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Explore a problem space before planning. Proposes architectural approaches with trade-offs, challenges the obvious solution, estimates impact, and produces a decision document. Code is the LAST thing we touch — this skill never writes code or plans. ([nikhilw/structured-agentic-workflow](https://github.com/nikhilw/structured-agentic-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Generate creative options for an advanced producer: track concepts, hooks, drops, breakdowns, transitions, sound directions, references. Use when the user says "brainstorm a track", "give me ideas for", "I'm stuck on", "what should I do for the drop", "throw directions at me", or wants options rather than execution. House/EDM by default. ([patrickking67/producer](https://github.com/patrickking67/producer/tree/HEAD/plugins/producer/skills/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Brainstorm [topic] [--target P] [--rounds N=2] [--mid-pct P=20] [--lenses generic|optimization] [--cross-model] [--fable] [--out FILE] [+Nk]. Multi-agent fan-out: ideate across diverse lenses (mixed STRONG/MID model tiers, mixed effort) → adversarially refute → debate → synthesize a ranked report. Use when the user asks to "brainstorm", "give me several ideas/options for X", "ways to optimize/speed up X", or "explore approaches to X". This is the DEFAULT brainstorm variant — prefer it unless the user explicitly says "mythos" or asks for the all-Fable variant (then use brainstorm-mythos). ([radumarias/xorio-claude-plugin](https://github.com/radumarias/xorio-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Gate de design para desenvolvimento Fluig. Use ANTES de criar qualquer artefato (widget, form, dataset, workflow). Entrevista o desenvolvedor, mapeia integrações e produz um design aprovado antes de acionar qualquer skill de scaffolding. Invoque ao iniciar qualquer nova funcionalidade Fluig. ([tbc-servicos/dataagile-agent-kit](https://github.com/tbc-servicos/dataagile-agent-kit/tree/HEAD/fluig/skills/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Planejamento de desenvolvimento ADVPL/TLPP — intake de MIT044, explora o projeto, faz perguntas, propõe abordagens e gera design aprovado antes de qualquer código. Encadeia /protheus:plan. ([tbc-servicos/dataagile-agent-kit](https://github.com/tbc-servicos/dataagile-agent-kit/tree/HEAD/protheus/skills/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Use when a creative prompt, design question, or open-ended problem needs multiple independent adversarial ideas. ([tony/skills](https://github.com/tony/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/weave/skills/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Use BEFORE planning, when an idea is still fuzzy and needs discovery. Reach a precise, shared understanding of exactly what to build by interrogating the user relentlessly — fleshing out every requirement, detail, and gap — then settle it into a spec (problem, objectives, acceptance criteria, non-goals, UI direction). The spec is the *output* of that understanding, not a shortcut to it; don't rush it. TRIGGER when: user says "thinking about", "what if", "exploring", "noodling on", "should we", "wondering about", "feeling out", "kicking around", "let's iterate on", or invokes /brainstorm expli… ([trailhead-ai/trailhead](https://github.com/trailhead-ai/trailhead/tree/HEAD/tools/craft/plugins/craft/skills/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Use when brainstorming software solutions, evaluating architectural approaches, debating technical decisions, or exploring feature ideas before implementation. Acts as a senior architect — challenges assumptions, presents 2-3 viable alternatives with pros/cons, and gives brutally honest feedback under YAGNI/KISS/DRY. Triggers on "/ck:brainstorm", "should we use X or Y", "how should we approach X", "is this worth the complexity", or any high-level architecture/feasibility debate. Does NOT implement — advise only. ([trungdo9/ClauKit](https://github.com/trungdo9/ClauKit/tree/HEAD/skills/software/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Inspect the full diff and surrounding architecture, then propose high-leverage improvements without editing files. ([vasyapym/vasyapym.github.io](https://github.com/vasyapym/vasyapym.github.io/tree/HEAD/skills/productivity/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: Generative ideation partner that explores multiple approaches to a problem or feature without writing anything or consulting the knowledge base. Use when the user wants options generated before any decision exists, e.g. "brainstorm approaches for implementing websocket reconnection logic", "what are some ways to solve X". ([vivantel/kms](https://github.com/vivantel/kms/tree/HEAD/plugins/kms/skills/brainstorm) · ★3)
- **brainstorm**: 产品概念创意构思。通过情感锚点、品味画像、约束识别，生成多个产品概念并验证范围与可行性，产出 product-concept.md。 ([zls3434/Software-Engineering-Studios](https://github.com/zls3434/Software-Engineering-Studios/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/brainstorm) · ★3 · 12 copies)
- **brainstorm**: Ideation organ upstream of /envision: talk a half-formed idea into a name, one-line description, edges (in/out) and end-goal, and write a light .claude/ideas/&lt;slug&gt;.md note that /envision picks up. Converges rather than chats. Use on /brainstorm [slug], "help me shape this idea", or when the user has a vague idea to shape. Mode A — propose-confirm, never auto-fires. ([AcKeskin/contexture](https://github.com/AcKeskin/contexture/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorm) · ★2)
- **brainstorm**: Facilitates open-ended feature brainstorming. Use when the user wants to explore new ideas, discuss product direction, or think through possibilities without committing to anything yet. No files are modified during the brainstorm. ([asboyer/continuity-template](https://github.com/asboyer/continuity-template/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/brainstorm) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **brainstorm**: Use when the user explicitly asks for brainstorming, pre-ticket discovery, fuzzy product shaping, visual option exploration, or turning an idea into an approved design before implementation planning. ([ayberkcansever/ai-skills](https://github.com/ayberkcansever/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/sdlc/brainstorm) · ★2)
- **brainstorm**: Helps the user generate and pressure-test ideas or options for a decision, with honest tradeoffs rather than a wall of suggestions. ([broomva/life](https://github.com/broomva/life/tree/HEAD/runtime-skills/brainstorm) · ★2)
- **brainstorm**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "brainstorm", "refine an idea", "flesh out a feature", "define requirements". Guides interactive refinement of rough ideas into concise, actionable feature specifications. Focuses on the "what" and architecture — never the "how". ([Conava/claude-devline](https://github.com/Conava/claude-devline/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorm) · ★2)
- **brainstorm**: Free-form ideation that investigates context, clarifies goals, explores approaches, validates direction, then routes into QRSPI (`/q-question` or `/q-outline`). Use when the user wants open-ended brainstorming before entering the staged workflow. ([CoreyCole/dotfiles](https://github.com/CoreyCole/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.pi-config/agent/skills/brainstorm) · ★2)
